**Layout**

Three horizontal bands: a light grey header band (0 to 20.5% of slide height) holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%, a white content band (20.5% to 86.1%) holding a full-width line chart with its legend at upper left, and a light grey footer band (86.1% to 100%) with the source line at left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and page number right-aligned to x 94.4%-95.6%. Left margin is about 20% of the slide width; the chart plot runs from about x 20.5% to 95.6%. The title sits in the header, the page number 88 sits at the bottom right of the footer.

**Visuals**

One line chart. Title: US VC deal activity ($bn), bold sans-serif above the plot at upper left. Two series drawn as lines with round markers: Under $50m in dark grey (about #4D4D4D) and Over $50m in red (about #EF4236). Y axis: $bn, linear, range 0 to 200, labelled 0, 50, 100, 150, 200, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at 50, 100, 150 and 200 and a solid thin grey baseline at 0. X axis: categories 2010, 2011, 2012, 2013, 2014, 2015, 2016, 2017, 2018, 2019, 2020, Q3 2021 TTM, evenly spaced. No values are printed on the points; estimates follow. Under $50m (about): 2010 26, 2011 31, 2012 33, 2013 36, 2014 44, 2015 47, 2016 46, 2017 51, 2018 58, 2019 63, 2020 64, Q3 2021 TTM 84. Over $50m (about): 2010 7, 2011 16, 2012 10, 2013 14, 2014 32, 2015 39, 2016 37, 2017 38, 2018 86, 2019 80, 2020 102, Q3 2021 TTM 199. Legend at upper left of the chart, stacked vertically: dark grey dot with label Under $50m above red dot with label Over $50m. Highlighted points: none marked specifically; the Over $50m point at Q3 2021 TTM is the highest value on the chart and sits just below the 200 gridline. Source: NVCA. The chart is not cropped, boxed or tinted; it sits directly on the white band.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from 20.5% to 86.1% of slide height. Flat, minimal look. Header and footer band colour light grey #F1F1F1; content band white #FFFFFF. Title in a light, high-contrast serif (Photina-like) in dark grey #545454, around 5.6% of slide height in regular weight. Subtitle in the same serif, dark grey #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and all chart labels, legend and source line in Helvetica Neue sans-serif, regular weight except the chart title in bold, dark grey #545454 to #535353, about 1.85% of slide height for labels and 2.2% for the chart title. Author name in Helvetica Neue Bold and date and page number in Helvetica Neue regular, light grey #A6A6A6, 1.85% of slide height. Lines are about 2.5 px thick at slide scale, with markers about 1.3% of slide width in diameter. Gridlines are thin dotted light grey. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

One line chart. Type: line chart with circular markers, single red series. Title: US tech venture investing ($bn, 2021 dollars). Y-axis: $bn, 2021 dollars, range 0 to about 125, labelled ticks at 0, 25, 50, 75, 100, 125, with dotted horizontal gridlines at each tick. X-axis: years 1980 to 2020, labelled every ten years (1980, 1990, 2000, 2010, 2020), one point per year (41 points). No legend. ### Slide 90

**Text**

Hot money / Source: Fred Wilson / Benedict Evans — January 2022 / 90 / AVC / NOV 15, 2021 / Seed Rounds At $100mm Post Money / We have been seeing quite a few seed rounds getting done in and around $100mm post-money and that concerns me for a few reasons: / Seed stage is when a company has a good team, a good idea, but has not yet proven product market fit and a go to market model, and has not yet demonstrated a sustainable business model. / These investments have a high failure rate. In my experience, roughly half of seed stage investments fail completely, wiping out everyone's investment, including the founding team's. / There is a lot of dilution from the seed round to exit, in my experience, a seed investor will be diluted by around 2/3 between seed and exit. / A power law distribution exists in outcomes in any early stage portfolio and a seed portfolio is no differernt. / avc.com

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: the left half (0 to 50% width) is a light grey panel holding the title 'Hot money' at upper left (top about 24%, left margin about 4.6%) and the source line at lower left (about 91% from top). The right half is white, holding a single browser-window mockup centred horizontally at about 58% to 95% width and 11% to 89% height. Footer line 'Benedict Evans — January 2022' and page number 90 sit at the bottom right on the same baseline (about 91% from top). Text is left-aligned in the left column; the footer is right-aligned toward the page-number margin.

**Visuals**

A screenshot mockup of a web browser window showing the blog AVC (avc.com), boxed with a soft drop shadow beneath it. The browser chrome is light grey (#F0F0F0-like) with three small traffic-light dots at top left (red #FF5F57, yellow #FEBC2E, green #28C840), a rounded address bar reading 'avc.com' with a padlock icon and reload icon, and a small document icon. The page content is white: a green hamburger menu icon (#5E8B1F-like, three bars) at left, the bold black wordmark 'AVC' centred, and a small circular cartoon avatar of a smiling person with green background at right. A thin grey rule sits below the header. Beneath are the date, headline, intro paragraph and a four-item bulleted list in a serif body face. No photographs, charts or diagrams beyond the avatar and icons; no cropping of the mockup.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left half solid light grey #F2F2F2, right half solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 92

**Text**

First 50 years, second 50 years
Tech used to sell accounting tools to big companies - now it’s part of our life
Mainframes
Big companies
PCs
All companies
Web
Middle-classes
Smartphones
Everyone
Systemically
important part of
society
Benedict Evans
–– January 2022
92

**Layout**

Full-width slide with a light grey header band and footer band framing a white middle band. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at x about 20.1%, starting at the top margin (title top at y 5.5%). The white band (y 20.5% to 86.1%) is divided into five vertical white columns separated by thin grey gaps, with the leftmost 20% of the band left empty; the column labels are centred under the arrows at the bottom of each column. The footer credit and page number sit at the bottom right at y 91.1%.

**Visuals**

Five curved S-shaped arrows, one per column, rising from lower left to upper right, each with a filled triangular arrowhead at its end. Arrow 1 (Mainframes): solid grey line from about x 20.0%, y 64.0% to an arrowhead at about x 34.7%, y 53.0%. Arrow 2 (PCs): solid grey line from about x 35.1%, y 58.6% to an arrowhead at about x 49.6%, y 47.5%. Arrow 3 (Web): solid grey line from about x 50.0%, y 53.0% to an arrowhead at about x 64.6%, y 42.0%. Arrow 4 (Smartphones): solid grey line from about x 65.0%, y 47.5% to an arrowhead at about x 79.8%, y 36.5%. Arrow 5 (Systemically important part of society): red dotted line from about x 80.2%, y 40.8% rising to a red arrowhead at about x 95.0%, y 29.8%. Line weight is about 0.4% of slide height, grey #808080 for arrows 1 to 4 and red #EB483F for arrow 5. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 with a white centre band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Light grey background #F1F1F1 with a white centre band. Title and body text are set in a high-contrast serif (PhotinaMT, close to a transitional serif such as Baskerville), regular weight. Title in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height; subtitle in dark grey #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Column labels in dark grey #545454, about 3.0% of slide height, centred. The final label and its accent arrow are red #EB483F. Footer in Helvetica Neue, grey #A6A6A6, about 1.9% of slide height; the name is bold. Shapes are plain rectangles with square corners and no shadows; thin grey #F1F1F1 gaps separate the columns.

### Slide 93

**Text**

The last time this happened
GM, Ford and Chrysler were 20% of Fortune 500 earnings
in the late 1950s
But who made more from the deployment of cars, and who
made more from car-commerce - Detroit, or retailers?

Benedict Evans
–– January 2022
93

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A light grey text panel fills the left half, with the title and two body paragraphs left-aligned at about 4.6% from the left edge, starting at about 24% of the height. The source line sits near the bottom left at about 91% of the height. The right half is a full-height painted illustration bleeding off the top, right and bottom edges. A footer row along the bottom of the left panel carries the author name, date and page number at about 91% of the height.

**Visuals**

One illustration, placed in the right half. It is a 1950s-style painted aerial view of a large single-storey Sears store with a flat roof and pale cream and grey facade, set in a vast car park. The roof has a 'Sears' script sign and the facade has a second 'Sears' sign in yellow-outlined script. Rows of parked cars in mixed colours (greens, browns, greys, pinks, yellows) fill the foreground and lower right. Shoppers walk along the pavement and lawns, and light poles stand in the car park. Beyond are rows of houses, a distant range of hills and a flat plain. The sky is a dusky pink-mauve with orange-yellow glow near the horizon, and the scene is lit warmly in late day light. The image is cropped to a tall rectangle that runs full height of the slide, bleeding off the top, bottom and right edges, with no border or overlay. Dominant colours are dusty pink #B8787A, cream #E8D5B0, grey-green #6E7F6A and muted slate #8A8F99.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F2F2F2 on the left half; white #FFFFFF behind the right half, covered by the photograph. Left panel background is light grey #F2F2F2 with no gradient or texture. Title is set in a light-weight book serif (PhotinaMT-like) in dark grey #545454 at about 5.6% of slide height. Body text is the same serif, regular weight, dark grey #545454 at about 3.0% of slide height, with a blank line between the two paragraphs. Source line is a regular neo-grotesque sans (Helvetica Neue) in #545454 at about 1.9% of height. Footer name is Helvetica Neue Bold and the date and page number are Helvetica Neue regular, all in light grey #A6A6A6 at about 1.9% of height. Accent colour: none. Shapes: the right panel is a plain white #FFFFFF rectangle with no border, shadow or rounded corners, fully covered by the photograph. No lines or icons.
